STATE BANK OF INDIA 
Central Office 

NOTICE 

Bombay, the 24 th August 1972 

In pursuance of Regulation 76 (1) of the State Bank 
of India General Regulations 1955, the Executive Com- 
mittee of the Central Board has empowered the incum- 
bent of the post of Chief Manager, Commercial Division 
at Bombay Main Branch to exercise the signing powers 
specified therein, with effect from the 1st January 1973. 

By order of the Executive Committee 
of the Central Board 

T. R. VARADACHARY 
Managing Director 


STATE BANK OF PATIALA 
Head Office 

NOTICE 

Patiala, the 2nd April, 1973 
S.B.O.P. 14. — The following transfers and changes in 
the posting of Supervising Staff are hereby notified : — 

1. Shri Piara Singh, Officer Grade II to be Assistant 
Accountant, Bali Nagar, Delhi Branch as from 
the close of business on 26-2-1973, 

2. Shri D. R. Singla, Officer Grade II (on proba- 
tion) to be Assistant Accountant, Pusa Road, 
Delhi as from the commencement of business on 
1-3-1973, 

3. Shri D, C. Gupta, Officer Grade 1 held charge of 
Shardanand Marg, Delhi Branch as from the 
close of business on 19-1-1973 to the commence- 
ment of business on 22-1-1973, 

S. D. GANDA 
General Manager

MINISTRY OF COMMERCE 
Textiles Committee 

Bombay- 18, the 21 st April 1973 

No. 33 (5) /73-AD.— In exercises of the powers con- 
ferred by section 23 of the Textiles Committee Act, 1963 
(41 of 1963), the Textiles Committee hereby makes, with 
the previous sanction of the Central Government, the 
following regulations to amend the Textiles Committee’s 
Employees (Medical Benefits) Regulations, 1968, 
namely ■. — 

1. These Regulations may be called the Textiles Com- 
mittee’s Employees (Medical Benefits) Amendment 
Regulations, 1973, 


2. In Regulation 5 of the Textiles Committee’s Em- 
ployees (Medical Benefits) Regulations, 1968 : 

(i) for the words and figures "Half month’s pay 
subject to a minimum of Rs, 100/- and maxi- 
mum of Rs. 500/-’’, the words and figures "One 
month’s pay subject to a minimum of Rs, 200/- 
and a maximum of Rs. 1,000/-” shall be substi- 
tuted; 

(ii) for the words and figures "Half month’s pay 
subject to a minimum of Rs. 50/- and maxi- 
mum of Rs, 150/-”, the words and figures 
"One month’s pay subject to a minimum of 
Rs, 100/- and a maximum of Rs. 300/-” shall 
be substituted. 

P. N. ANAND 
Secretary
